# UNCERTAINTIES

Explicit knowledge holes. Every `[UNCERTAIN U-NNNN]` marker in a note or source must have a row here.
Types: `semantic` / `structural` block C3 for that address; `behavioural` / `tooling` do not.

| ID | addr | type | description | path to resolution | status |
|----|------|------|-------------|--------------------|--------|
| U-0001 | 0x0045baf0 | tooling | `golf_clean.exe` loads `jgld.dll` (Jackal **debug** build, `C:\JackDev\Debug\jgld.pdb`), not the release `jgl.dll` shipped beside it. Why, and whether the release DLL is a drop-in, is not established. | Find the `LoadLibraryA("jgld.dll")` call site in Ghidra, read the selection logic; A/B boot with `jgl.dll` substituted. | open |

Cause not established. | Not needed for the RE target; revisit only if the protected exe is ever needed as a reference. | open |
| U-0003 | 0x004722c0 | behavioural | Organic chain established (2026-10-02): the notice is called at 0x004667e1 inside FUN_00466370(golfer, force), the golfer-pair story advance, only when the golfer's story stage (short +0xb2, 0x0057956a) and its partner's (partner = short +0xa2) are both 4 after the increment; the story id (short +0xb0, 0x00579568) is the 2nd argument. Gates that return 0: story id == -1; force == 0 and hole (char +0x21) < 2 and byte +0x22 == 0; both stages > 3; force == 0 and counter (short +0xb4) <= stage*4+4; golfer stage > partner stage after the increment (rolled back, counter -= counter/3). The organic caller with force=0 is 0x00428cc8 in FUN_004289e0 (pair flag 0x100000, same hole, distance gate); force=1 calls come from 0x0041c502/0x0041e9c7 in FUN_0040f5c0. The counter is updated per hole at 0x00428892 in FUN_00427380 as `c = c*7/8 + short(+0xa4)`. Runtime (scenario golfer_stories, 4-hole course, x32): chapters advance (return 1) and a pair reached stage 3; on the 1-hole course all calls return 0 (hole 1 gate). +0xa4 is the golfer's mood: FUN_00467a00(golfer, thought, arg) adds a per-thought delta (switch at 0x00467c51, -3..+1) and clamps it with FUN_00467130(mood, -10, 10) after LAB_0046868c; the thought text comes from FUN_00469b00 (2 = 'Darn, I'm in the rough!', 8 = 'Who designed this ... course!', 9 = 'Hey, that ball almost hit me!', 0x1c = 'Look at that lovely ...', 0x1d = 'I like the variety on this course'). Spawn mood is rand+3..5 (0x00421bc0). At hole end the counter becomes mood itself when the pair flag 0x100000 is clear, so > 16 needs the paired 7/8 average. FUN_004289e0 also requires both partners inside the screen rect x 0x32..0x2ee, y 0x32..0x1c2 (golfer +0x08/+0x0c). Runtime, 4-hole course, 180 s: top mood losses ids 3, 8, 0x24, 9, 2; one paired golfer reached counter 19. Pinning mood to 10 (log/probe/mood_pin.js, 480 s) did not pass stage 2. Fixtures 2026-10-03: 5 holes (best stage 3, 25 min x32); 9 holes zoomed out (stage 1: off-rect); 9 holes with the camera tile written to (24, 24) at 0x4c2ba0/a4 (best stage 2; in 46 of 49 samples no story pair was on the course, so pair arrivals are now the limit on that course). Arrivals: FUN_0040f5c0 calls the allocator FUN_00421bc0(1) when fewer than 2 golfers wait (or every 0x80 ticks), fewer than 8 wait and flag 0x200000 of 0x59e7b8 is clear; pairs start (FUN_0045de80 at 0x40fe46) only when hole 1 is free. FUN_00421bc0 tries up to 999 random golfer types 1..75 and accepts one only if no waiting/on-course golfer has the same type or type % 19, its record in 0x5849e0 (stride 0x2c) has +0x29 != -1 and, for param 1, +2 != 0; otherwise it writes 'Your membership is declining' and returns -1. Runtime on stories9.sve: >99% of calls return -1 (e.g. 3422 calls, 3408 failures in 45 s); +2 is set for 12 of 75 types (stories5.sve: 20), so the member count caps arrivals. Story choice in FUN_0045de80: up to 3 random picks among available stories (0x53a454, stride 0x32; 10 available, ids 0..18 even, constant over 9 min), no duplicate on the course, then cleared when range(0x561258) > 6 (0x561258 = happy endings so far, 0 here). +2 is the membership level (low 3 bits; join/upgrade FUN_00406670 via FUN_00427380's round-end points, resign in FUN_004289e0 zeroes levels > 1): re/analysis/golfers/00406670_membership.md. On stories9.sve membership stays ~13 types, so arrivals stay capped; joins and arrivals stop because golfers whose mood drops below 0 quit and their type gets +0x29 = 0xff for good (only a happy ending rewrites it): blocked types 0 -> 15 in 220 s on stories9.sve. The organic landmark therefore needs a course that keeps golfer moods >= 0. 2026-10-04: mood losses are the rough (thought 2), the bad-lie complaint (8), balls near golfers (9), tantrum reactions (0x24) and the hole rating of FUN_0042dea0 (flag 4 too hard, 8 too easy: thought 0x13 becomes 0x17, -1, on a bad score there). mood5.sve (5 holes of ~90 yd with fairways) kept 8-16 golfers on the course for 903 s at x32 (tee+green courses: empty in ~200 s), but bans still reached 27, 4 of 5 holes were rated too easy, the best story stage was 2 and 0x4722c0 was not called. Rollback cause (re/analysis/golfers/004669f0_story_reply.md, formula checked 44/44 at runtime): on each advance FUN_004669f0 rewrites the partner's chapter as min(cap - 1, (max(mood, counter) - 1) / 2) + 1 if the partner's latest non-0x13 thought raised its mood (-1/-2 if it lowered it), or cap when a Landmark (placed type 4, state < 16, state & 3 == 3) is within 3.7-7.7 tiles; the advance is undone when the partner ends below. Chapter 4 therefore needs partner max(mood, counter) >= 7 plus a happy latest thought, or such a landmark. On mood5.sve 51 of 100 advance attempts rolled back, 18 stopped at the counter gate. Not reached organically: both partners at stage 4. | Longer par 3s with fairways so no hole is rated too easy or too hard (FUN_0042dea0 thresholds), decorations (0x1c), >= 5 holes on screen; raise partner moods to >= 7 (positive thoughts: 0x1c near landmarks, 0x1d variety, 0x22) or get a landmark with state & 3 == 3 near the holes. | partial |

| U-0008 | 0x0040f5c0 | behavioural | RESOLVED (c3fy2, 2026-10-16). The c3fy reimplementation of mainLoop (shim/src/re/c3fy.cpp) diverged on course_season: 2 bytes at hole_open (hole-1 flags +0x201 at 0x00575eb9; golfer-type 69 byte +3 at 0x004df79b), then golfers / story pairs / rng_seed by season_060 and a mid-season crash. Cause: transcription defects in P3, not the original. (1) 0x00413a59 `cmp eax, edi; mov eax, [0x5685f0]; jge`: the C code reloaded eax before evaluating the jump, so the downhill flag 0x2000 compared the hole index with the tee height - 1 (frame text "a scenic 175 yard downhill par 3" in the ON arm only; write watch: the ON arm sets the bit every frame from T=13837 at WINMM.dll+0x1181c2 = the transcribed 0x00413a77); the uphill test 0x004139ee..0x004139f5 had the same fault. (2) 0x00414a88 `test eax, eax; lea eax, [ecx*8]; jne`: the jump read the lea, not typeBit7Clear's result, so the byte +3 of the golfer-type record took `bl << 4` instead of `(bl - 5) << 4` (0x00414a93 / 0x00414aab). With those fixed, hole_open was SAME and per-poll traces (scenario_ab.py --trace / --watch) located the season divergence at (3) 0x00414e91 `jne`, reached only from the `jge` at 0x00414e11 after `cmp cl, 0xb` (0x00414e0e), written as `eax != 0`: golfer animation bytes +0x16 / +0x17 were reset to (0, [F+0x4c]) at 0x00415330 / 0x00415337 (first difference T=17877, lasting from T=42925, rng_seed from T=45652). Five more defects of the same classes were found by the other scenarios and by three audits of the listing (log/c3/c3fy2_hazards.py, c3fy2_setter_check.py, c3fy2_strcheck.py): 0x004156b7 setle (flags reloaded), 0x0041e38f (eax not loaded with [0x00824130] before the switch at 0x0041e6f9: golfer_events selected golfer 4 after the panel closed), 0x0041cabc and 0x0041ec50 (strcpy idioms written as strcat: ui_tour saved Themes\Championship\<course> without the overwrite prompt and crashed in writeCourseInfo's unchecked fopen). After the fixes, 9 scenarios are GREEN (state + frames, mainLoop ON vs OFF, two OFF runs identical each) and both negctl modes are RED. Evidence and runs: log/c3/c3fy2_notes.txt. | Closed. Open coverage questions (paths no scenario reaches) are listed in log/c3/c3fy2_notes.txt. | resolved |

| U-0018 | 0x00404a14 | tooling | `golf_clean.exe` inter-function padding holds 716 call instructions no flow reaches (358 `call rel32`, 358 `call [IAT]`, one per code cave of 5..13 bytes after a `ret`; rel32 displacement = 0x00405099 minus the next entry) and a three-piece dispatcher 0x00405099 -> 0x00404a14 -> [0x00403f9c] = 0x008a2590, inside the range SecServ.dll occupied (0x00850000..0x0091F000) in the logged Safedisc2Cleaner runs. ORIGIN MEASURED (b4, re/analysis/data/safedisc_padding.md): SafeDisc protector output written before encryption. Above 0x00425990 the wrapped `golf.exe` .text decrypts as `c = p XOR h(previous 4 plaintext bytes)` (99.90% agreement); there 566 of 619 slots are present in the encrypted file byte for byte, 0 conflict in kind, 14 + 4 recover no slot byte. `golf_v100_clean.exe` (SafeDisc 2.51.21, same cleaner build) holds 183 `call [IAT]` slots of the same kind (filler = copy of the bytes before the slot), drawn independently per build (49 shared positions, 0 shared imports); jgld/Terrain/sound/binkw32 hold none. No instruction in a listed function and no data word references the slots or the dispatcher (5 ASCII coincidences), and xref.py / port_survey_globals.py / undiscovered.py / pointer_callers.py do not count them: the port ignores them. STILL OPEN: (a) whether the dispatcher and the 97 slots below 0x00425990 were in the encrypted file (that region uses another keystream); (b) which writer changed the IAT operand of 35 `call [IAT]` slots after encryption (e.g. 0x004851a6 wrapped [0x004ba1e0], cleaned [0x004ba1c8]; real call sites unchanged), SafeDisc at load or the cleaner's import pass. | Decrypt the region below 0x00425990 (its keystream), or take an in-memory image of the wrapped exe after SafeDisc decrypts .text and before its import setup, and compare the dispatcher, the 97 slots and the 35 operands with `golf_clean.exe`. | partial |

The 48 left are centres exactly on a vertex (40) or on a diagonal edge (8), where the real GL's choice follows no direction rule (both signs occur for the same edge direction): a fixed-point edge walker's rounding, not modelled. A playing host therefore blocks in those pumps: idle passes do not happen in a live session (the title menu's hover drawing, showHoleStatsScreen's clock-driven page turns between inputs). Paced loops (pumpAndRedraw, the credits) and the frame body's pumps may decline.
